         liabilities less cash and bank balances) divided by equity attributable to             負債減現金及銀行結餘）除以股東應佔權益計算。
         shareholders as at year end.

    CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
    主席報告

    Dear Shareholders,                                                                              致各位股東：

    O n b e h a l f o f t h e b o a r d o f d i r e c t o r s (t h e “B o a r d”) o f D a p h n e   本人謹代表達芙妮國際控股有限公司（「本公司」）董
    International Holdings Limited (the “Company”) together with its                                事會（「董事會」）連同其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」），
    subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”), I am pleased                            向各位提呈本公司截至二零二五年十二月三十一日
    to present the annual report of the Company for the year ended                                  止年度之年報。
    31 December 2025.

    In 2025, the global economy grappled with persistent geopolitical                               二零二五年，全球經濟持續面對地緣政治緊張局勢、
    tensions, shifting trade dynamics, and supply chain reconfigurations.                           貿易格局變化以及供應鏈重塑的挑戰。儘管通脹有
    While inflation moderated, economic recovery remained uneven                                    所緩和，但經濟復蘇仍不均衡且脆弱。中國順利完
    and fragile. China concluded its 14th Five-Year Plan on solid                                   成「十四五」規劃，以堅實的基礎實現穩健收官，國
    footing, with gross domestic product (GDP) expanding 5.0% to                                    內生產總值(GDP)增長5.0%，突破人民幣140萬億元
    exceed RMB140 trillion – a testament to robust fundamentals and                                 大關，彰顯了中國經濟扎實的基本面和積極的政策
    proactive policy support. Yet consumer confidence remained subdued,                             支援。然而，消費者信心依然偏弱，促使消費者在購
    prompting shoppers to seek both value-for-money and emotional                                   買決策中既追求性價比，也渴望情感共鳴。本集團迅
    connection in their purchases. The Group responded with agility,                                速應對，以更清晰的戰略重點推進雙品牌戰略，通
    advancing its dual-brand strategy with a sharper strategic focus. We                            過更精準的品牌定位與轉型措施，強化了「達芙妮」
    strengthened “DAPHNE” brand equity through refined positioning and                              的品牌價值，同時將「達芙妮.實驗室」打造為文化先
    transformation initiatives, while cultivating “DAPHNE.LAB” brand as                             鋒，通過大膽的跨界合作與沉浸式敘事，吸引了注
    a cultural vanguard that captivated style-conscious young consumers                             重時尚的年輕消費群體。以韌性為基石，我們實施
    through bold collaborations and immersive storytelling. With resilience                         更嚴格的分銷管控、完善的授權體系以及更精簡的
    as our cornerstone, we optimised our brand licensing, wholesale and                             供應鏈，持續優化品牌授權、批發及直營零售業務
    direct retail portfolio through stricter distribution controls, refined                         佈局。多項戰略措施共同推動本集團在波動的零售
    licensing framework, and leaner supply chains. Together, these                                  環境中實現穩健增長，奠定可持續、高品質的發展
    strategic moves delivered stable growth amid volatile retail dynamics,                          基礎，並進一步鞏固我們在女鞋行業的領先地位。
    positioning the Group for sustainable, high-quality development and
    enduring leadership in the women’s footwear industry.

    As livestream shopping and Chinese brands consumption surged,                                   隨著直播購物與中國品牌消費熱潮的興起，中國電
    China’s e-commerce sector continued to thrive, with content-driven                              商行業持續繁榮，其中內容驅動型電商正逐漸成為

     on deposits generally offered by commercial banks, and would not affect     因此，管理層認為購買金融投資屬公平合理，並符
     the working capital or the normal business operation of the Group. As       合本集團及本公司股東的整體利益。
     such, management is of the view that the purchases of the financial
     investments are fair and reasonable and in the interests of the Group and
     the shareholders of the Company as a whole.

Foreign Exchange Risk Management                                              外匯風險管理
Foreign exchange risk arises from commercial transactions and                 外匯風險來自於商業交易和以本集團實體經營的功
recognised assets and liabilities denominated in currencies other than        能貨幣以外的貨幣計值的已確認資產及負債。本集
the functional currency of the Group’s entities to which they operate.        團主要面對與港元及美元有關的外匯風險。
The Group is mainly exposed to foreign exchange risk with respect to
Hong Kong Dollars and United States Dollars.

The Group manages its foreign exchange risk by performing regular             本集團管理外匯風險透過定期審閱本集團之淨外匯
reviews of the Group’s net foreign exchange exposures and the Group           風險，且於預期相關外幣出現大幅波動時，本集團
may use forward foreign exchange contracts when major fluctuation             或會使用遠期外匯合約。於截至二零二五年十二月
in the relevant foreign currency is anticipated. During the year ended        三十一日止年度，本集團並未簽訂任何遠期外匯合
31 December 2025, the Group did not enter into any foreign exchange           約以對外匯風險進行對沖安排。外幣兌換人民幣之
forward contract to hedge the foreign exchange risk exposure. Any             任何重大匯率波動可能會對本集團造成財務影響。
significant exchange rate fluctuations of foreign currencies against
Renminbi may have had financial impact on the Group.

Significant Investments                                                       重大投資

    The Board is responsible for overall management and control                董事會負責本公司之整體管理及監控，包
    of the Company, including the approval and monitoring of                   括批准及監督一切政策上之事宜、整體策

               Summary of Board Diversity Policy                                            董事會多元化政策概要
               The Company has adopted a board diversity policy (the                        本公司已採納董事會多元化政策（「董事會
               “Board Diversity Policy”) which sets out the approach to                     多元化政策」），當中載有達致及維持董事
               achieve and maintain diversity on the Board in order to                      會多元化以提升董事會效率之方法。
               enhance the effectiveness of the Board.

               Pursuant to the Board Diversity Policy, the Company seeks                    根據董事會多元化政策，本公司在考慮委
               to achieve board diversity through having due regard to a                    任新董事時，透過考慮甄選標準中的多項
               number of diversity factors, including but not limited to                    多元化因素，包括但不限於性別、年齡、
               gender, age, cultural and education background, professional                 文化及教育背景、專業經驗、技術、知識
               experience, skills, knowledge and length of service, in the                  及 服 務 年 期，尋 求 達 致 董 事 會 成 員 多 元
               selection criteria in considering the appointment of new                     化。本公司亦將考慮有關自身商業模式及
               directors. The Company will also take into account factors                   不時的特殊需求等因素。最終將根據獲選
               relating to its own business model and specific needs from                   候選人士的優勢及可為董事會帶來的貢獻
               time to time. The ultimate decision is based on merit and                    而作決定。
               contribution that the selected candidates will bring to the
               Board.

          5.3 Risk Management and Internal Control                                5.3 風險管理及內部監控
               The Board is responsible for risk management (including              董事會負責本集團之風險管理（包括環境、
               environmental, social and governance-related risks) and              社會及管治相關風險）及內部監控系統及檢
               internal control systems of the Group and for reviewing              討其有效性。該系統的制定為管理而非消
               its effectiveness. Such systems are designed to manage               除未能實現經營目標之風險；對重大錯誤
               rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business        陳述或損失，只能提供合理的，而不是絕
               objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute         對的保證。
               assurance against material misstatement or loss.

               Aimed at providing reasonable assurance against material             本公司著力提供合理保證，避免出現重大
               errors, losses or fraud, the Company has established risk            錯誤、損失或欺詐，為此已成立風險管理
               management procedures which comprise the following                   程序，包括以下流程：
               process:

               •    Risk identification: identify major and significant risks       •   風險識別：識別可能影響達成本集團
                    that could affect the achievement of goals of the Group;            目標之主要及重大風險；

               •    Risk evaluation and assessment: assess and evaluate             •   風險評估及評核：根據已識別風險預
                    the identified risk according to its likely impact and the          料構成的影響及出現的可能性作出評
                    likelihood of occurrence; and                                       估及評核；及

               •    Risk management and mitigation: develop effective               •   風險管理及緩解：建立有效的監管活
                    control activities to mitigate the risks.                           動，務求緩解風險。

               In particular, procedures have been designed for safeguarding        具體而言，已制定程序保障資產不會在未
               assets against unauthorised use or disposition, ensuring the         獲授權下使用或處置；確保適當會計記錄
               maintenance of proper accounting records for the provision           的存置，為內部使用或公開發佈提供可靠
               of reliable financial information for internal use or for            財務資料；及確保遵守適用法律及法規。
               publication, and ensuring compliance with applicable laws
               and regulations.

               Risk identification and assessment are performed or updated          風險辨別及評估會每年進行或更新，記錄
               annually, and the results of risk assessment, evaluation and         各職能或營運之風險評估、評核及舒緩的
               mitigation of each function or operation are recorded and            結果，並供董事會及管理層審閱。
               communicated to the Board and management for reviews.

               An internal audit department has been established to conduct         本集團設有內部審核部門，以進行本集團
               risk management and internal audit of the Group. It performs         風險管理及內部審核。內部審核部門進行
               risk-based audits to review the effectiveness of the Group’s         基於風險的審核，審閱本集團之風險管理
               risk management and internal control systems so as to                及內部監控系統之有效性，以保證主要業
               provide assurance that key business and operational risks            務和營運之風險已被識別和管理。內部審
               are identified and managed. The work carried out by the              核部門每年最少向審核委員會報告兩次其
               internal audit department reports to the Audit Committee             開展之工作，並提供調查結果及建議。於
               with its findings and makes recommendations at least twice           截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度，
               a year. During the year ended 31 December 2025, the Board            董事會已就本集團之風險管理及內部監控
               has conducted a review of the effectiveness of the risk              系統之有效性進行檢討，包括財務、營運
               management and internal control systems of the Group,                及合規監控及風險管理職能。
               including financial, operational and compliance controls and
               risk management functions.

               During the year under review, the principal risks of the Group       於回顧年內，本集團的主要風險被識別並
               were identified and classified into strategic risks, operational     分類為可能對本集團造成重大影響的策略
               risks, financial risks and compliance risks that might have          風險、營運風險、財務風險及合規風險。
               significant impact on the Group. These risks included:               這些風險包括：

    (i)    Strategic risk                                                        (i)    策略風險

           implementation of its strategies, reviews the status of                      資源配置。
           strategic initiatives and makes timely adjustments to the
           allocation of resources as needed.

    (ii)   Operational risk                                                      (ii)   營運風險
           Operational risk refers to the risk that the Group                           營運風險是指本集團很大一部分營業
           heavily relies on a limited number of franchisees for a                      收入嚴重依賴有限數量的加盟商的風
           significant portion of the Group’s revenue.                                  險。

           reliable franchisees to minimise potential disruptions.                      的加盟商，以盡量減少潛在的干擾。

    (iii) Financial risk                                                         (iii) 財務風險

           unwilling or incapable of performing or repaying its                        完全履行或償還其義務，從而對本集
           obligations on time and in full, thereby result in loss to                  團造成損失的信用風險。
           the Group.

           The Group regularly evaluates the creditworthiness                           本集團定期評核客戶、供應商及金融
           of customers, suppliers and financial institutions, and                      機構的信用狀況，並根據客戶的信用
           establishes clear credit limits for each of the customers                    狀況及財務穩定性為每位客戶制定明
           based on their creditworthiness and financial stability.                     確的信用額度。本集團持續監控交易
           The Group continuously monitors the financial health                         對手的財務狀況和付款行為，以識別
           and payment behaviour of counterparties to identify                          潛在違約的早期跡象。
           early signs of potential default.

    (iv) Compliance risk                                                         (iv) 合規風險
           Compliance risk refers to the frequent changes in                          合規風險是指政府政策法規的頻繁變
           government policies and regulations which may have                         化可能對本集團的業務經營產生影響
           impact on the Group’s business operations and lead to                      並導致合規成本增加。
           increase in compliance cost.

    The Group from time to time monitors and analyses                            本集團不時監控和分析即將發生的政策變
    upcoming policy changes, regulatory updates and legislative                  化、監管更新和立法發展。此外，本集團

    operations, costs and profitability, and allocates a dedicated               分配專門預算，以避免意外的財務壓力。
    budget for compliance-related expenses to avoid unexpected
    financial strain.

       Disclosure of Inside Information                                                          披露內幕消息
       The Group has in place a policy on disclosure of inside                                   本集團備有內幕消息披露政策，載列處理

       and all relevant employees of the Group to ensure proper                                  僱員提供指引，確保有恰當的保護措施，

       statutory disclosure requirements. It also includes appropriate                           括適當的內部監控及申報系統，以辨別及
       internal control and reporting systems to identify and assess                             評估潛在的內幕消息。
       potential inside information.

       Key procedures in place include:                                                          已設有的主要步驟包括：

       •    defining the requirements of periodic financial and                                  •   向董事會及公司秘書界定定期財務及

            the confidentiality of the inside information before it is                               消息絕對保密；及
            properly disclosed to public; and

       •    p r o c e d u r e s o f c o m m u n i c a t i n g w i t h t h e G r o u p’s          •   與本集團持份者（包括股東、投資者、

            Rules.

     Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由於

     arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually       總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜合財務

     financial statements.

          in licensing, distribution and sale of footwear products and                產品及配件的授權許可、分銷及銷售業務。
          accessories in Chinese Mainland.

          Kong Limited. The address of its registered office is Cricket               之註冊辦事處位於Cricket Square, Hutchins Drive,

        2.2.1 Consolidation                                                                        2.2.1 綜合賬目
             The consolidated financial statements including the                                      綜合財務報表包括本公司及其所有附
             financial statements of the Company and all of its                                       屬公司截至十二月三十一日止之財務
             subsidiaries are made up to 31 December.                                                 報表。

             (i)   Subsidiaries                                                                       (i)   附屬公司
                   Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                                      附屬公司為所有本集團擁有控制
                   entities) over which the Group has control. The                                          權之實體（包括結構性實體）。當
                   Group controls an entity when the Group is                                               本集團通過控制該實體以得到或
                   exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from                                      有權得到不同的回報，及通過對
                   its involvement with the entity and has the ability to                                   該實體的權力影響該等回報時，
                   affect those returns through its power over the entity.                                  本集團則對該實體擁有控制權。

                   Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on                                     附屬公司由其控制權轉讓予本集
                   which control is transferred to the Group. They are                                      團之日起全面綜合入賬，並由該
                   deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                                        控制權終止之日起終止綜合入賬。

                   Inter-company transactions, balances and                                                 本集團內公司之間的交易、結餘
                   unrealised gains on transactions between group                                           及交易的未變現收益予以對銷。
                   companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are                                          未變現虧損亦予以撇除，除非該
                   also eliminated unless the transaction provided                                          交易提供所轉讓資產減值的證
                   evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.                                      據。附屬公司的會計政策已按需
                   Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been                                            要變更，以確保與本集團採用的
                   changed where necessary to ensure consistency                                            政策符合一致。
                   with the policies adopted by the Group.

                   the liabilities incurred to former shareholders of                                       本權益的公平價值。所轉讓代價

        2.2.16 Leases                                                              2.2.16 租賃
             Leases are recognised as right-of-use assets and                          租賃在租賃資產可供本集團使用當日
             corresponding liabilities at the date at which the leased                 確認為使用權資產和相應負債。
             asset is available for use by the Group.

             Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                            合同可能同時包含租賃和非租賃組成
             components. The Group allocates the consideration                         部分。本集團基於各租賃與非租賃組
             in the contract to the lease and non-lease components                     成部分的單獨價格相對比例分攤合同
             based on their relative stand-alone prices. However,                      對價。不過，對於本集團作為承租人
             for leases of real estate for which the Group is a lessee,                的房地產租賃，本集團已選擇不拆分
             it has elected not to separate lease and non-lease                        租賃與非租賃組成部分，而是將其作
             components and instead accounts for these as a single                     為一項單一的租賃組成部分進行會計
             lease component.                                                          處理。

             Assets and liabilities arising from leases are initially                  租賃產生的資產和負債按現值進行初
             measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities                      始計量。租賃負債包括以下租賃付款
             include the net present value of the following lease                      額的淨現值：
             payments:

             •     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed                        •       固定付款額（包括實質固定付款
                   payments), less any lease incentives receivable;                            額），扣除任何應收的租賃激勵；

             •     variable lease payments that are based on an index                  •       基於指數或比率確定的可變租賃
                   or a rate, initially measured using the index or rate                       付款額，採用租賃期開始日的指
                   as at the commencement date;                                                數或比率進行初始計量；

             •     amounts expected to be payable by the Group                         •       本集團根據餘值擔保預計應付的
                   under residual value guarantees;                                            金額；

             •     the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group                •       本集團合理確定將行使的購買選
                   is reasonably certain to exercise that option; and                          擇權的行使價；及

             •     payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if                 •       在租賃期反映出本集團將行使選
                   the lease term reflects the Group exercising that                           擇權的情況下終止租賃的罰款金
                   option.                                                                     額。

             Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain                        當本集團合理確定將行使續租選擇權
             extension options are also included in the measurement                    時，租賃付款額也納入負債的計量中。
             of the liability.

                    The lease payments are discounted using the interest                   租賃付款按租賃內含利率折現。本集
                    rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily             團的租賃內含利率通常無法直接確
                    determined, which is generally the case for leases in the              定，在此情況下，應採用承租人的增
                    Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,                量借款利率，即承租人在類似經濟環
                    being the rate that the individual lessee would have                   境下獲得與使用權資產價值接近的資
                    to pay to borrow the fund necessary to obtain an asset                 產，在類似期間以類似抵押條件借入
                    of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar                資金而必須支付的利率。
                    economic environment with similar terms, security and
                    conditions.

                    To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the                       為確定增量借款利率，本集團以個別
                    Group uses recent third-party financing received by                    承租人最近收到的第三方融資為起
                    the individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to                 點，並進行調整以反映融資條件自收
                    reflect changes in financing conditions since third party              到第三方融資後的變化。
                    financing was received.

                    Lease payments are allocated between principal and                     租賃付款額在本金和融資費用之間進
                    finance cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss            行分攤。融資費用在租賃期內計入損
                    over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic             益，以按照固定的週期性利率對各期
                    rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability             間負債餘額計算利息。
                    for each period.

                    Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the                使用權資產按成本計量，包括：
                    following:

                          liability;

                          commencement date less any lease incentives                          賃付款額扣除收到的租賃激勵；
                          received;

                    •     any initial direct costs; and                                    •   任何初始直接成本；及

                    •     restoration costs.                                               •   復原成本。

                    Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the                 使用權資產一般在資產的使用壽命與
                    shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on               租賃期兩者孰短的期間內按直線法計
                    a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain              提折舊。如本集團合理確定會行使購
                    to exercise a purchase option, the right-of-use asset is               買權，則在標的資產的使用壽命期間
                    depreciated over the underlying asset’s useful life.                   內對使用權資產計提折舊。

        2.2.18 Revenue recognition                                                2.2.18 收益確認
             (i)   Licensing fee income                                              (i)   許可權費收入
                   Licensing fee income represents revenues                                許可權費收入代表來自加盟商的
                   from franchisees. Revenue is recognised when                            收益。當本集團特許產品的控制
                   control of the Group’s authenticated products is                        權轉移到加盟商，而產品交付給
                   transferred to franchisees, being when the products                     加盟商後，收益將被確認。收益
                   are delivered to the franchisees. Revenue is                            是扣除授予加盟商的回扣之淨額
                   presented net of credits granted to franchisees. No                     列賬，並排除融資成份的存在。
                   element of financing is deemed present.

                   Rebates are awarded to franchisees based on                             授予加盟商的回扣乃根據其購買
                   volume of products purchased. It is recognised as                       產品數量。它被認為是從收益中
                   a deduction of revenue and the receipts in respect                      扣減的遞延收入，有關的收款在
                   of which are deferred and recognised as contract                        綜合資產負債表中確認為合約負
                   liabilities on the consolidated balance sheet.                          債。

             (ii) Sale of goods                                                      (ii) 貨品銷售
                  Sales are recognised when control of the products                       銷售於產品之控制權轉移時確
                  has transferred, being when the products are                            認，即貨品送抵客戶，且不存在
                  delivered to customers, and there is no unfulfilled                     可能影響客戶對產品接收的未履
                  obligation that could affect the customer                               行義務時予以確認。
                  acceptance of the products.

                   The goods are often sold with sales discounts.                          貨品銷售通常有銷售折扣。銷售
                   Revenue from these sales is recognised based                            收益是根據合同約定的價格扣除
                   on the price specified in the contract, net of the                      折扣後確認，並排除融資成分的
                   discounts. No element of financing is deemed                            存在。
                   present.

          2.3 Summary of other accounting policies                                    2.3 其他會計政策概要
               2.3.1 Offsetting financial instruments                                   2.3.1 抵銷金融工具
                    Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net                 倘有抵銷已確認金額的法定可執行權
                    amount is reported in the balance sheet when there                      力，且有意按淨額基準結算或變現資
                    is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised                 產並同時結算負債時，則金融資產與
                    amount and there is an intention to settle on a net                     負債將相互抵銷，淨額於資產負債表
                    basis or realise the asset and settle the liability                     內呈報。這法定可執行權力不能取決
                    simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not                  於將來發生之事件，但必須執行於日
                    be contingent on future events and must be enforceable                  常業務及當本集團或交易方出現違
                    in the normal course of business and in the event of                    約、無力償還或破產事件。
                    default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the Group or the
                    counterparty.

               2.3.2 Provisions                                                         2.3.2 撥備
                    Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                  倘本集團因過往事件而須承擔現有法
                    obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of past                  定或推定責任，而履行該責任時有可
                    events and it is probable that an outflow of economic                   能涉及經濟資源流失，並能可靠地衡
                    resources will be required to settle the obligation; and                量涉及金額，則確認有關撥備。不會
                    the amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are                  就未來經營虧損確認撥備。
                    not recognised for future operating losses.

                    Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                    倘有多項類似責任，於釐定履行有關
                    likelihood that an outflow of economic resources will                   責任而引致經濟資源流出之可能性
                    be required in settlement is determined by considering                  時，會整體考慮該等責任類別。即使
                    the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is                     同一責任類別所包含任何一個項目之
                    recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with                    資源流出之可能性極低，仍須確認撥
                    respect to any one item included in the same class of                   備。
                    obligations may be small.

                    Provisions are measured at the present value of the                     撥備乃按履行該責任預計所需開支以
                    expenditures expected to be required to settle the                      除稅前貼現率計算之現值計量，有關
                    obligations using a pre-tax that reflects current market                貼現率反映當時市場對貨幣時間值及
                    assessments of the time value of money and the risks                    該責任特定風險之評估。因時間流逝
                    specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision               而增加之撥備確認為利息支出。
                    due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.

               The Group’s business activities expose it to a variety of               本集團業務活動面臨多項財務風險：外匯

               the Group’s financial performance. The use of financial                 金融衍生工具管理若干風險需由董事會批

              To provide an indication about the reliability of the                    性，本集團將金融工具分為會計準則

              Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments traded                  第一層：在活躍市場交易的金融工具

              If all significant inputs required to fair value of the                  具的公平價值所需的所有重要資料都

              based on observable market data, the instruments are                     非基於可觀察的市場資料，則該工具
              included in level 3.                                                     被列入第三層。

4   CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES                                           4   重要會計估計及判斷
    AND JUDGEMENTS
    Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated and are based            本集團基於過往經驗及其他因素，包括經考慮
    on historical experience and other factors, including expectations          有關情況後合理預期日後將發生之事件，持續
    of future events that are believed to be reasonable under the               評估估計及判斷。
    circumstances.

    The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the                    本集團會對未來作出估計及假設。誠如其界定
    future. The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition,             涵義，所產生之會計估計甚少與有關之實際結
    seldom equal to the related actual result. The estimates and                果相同。有重大風險會對下個財政年度之資產
    assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material              及負債之賬面值造成大幅調整之估計及假設論
    adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities within         述如下。
    the next financial year are discussed below.

    4.1 Impairment of non-financial assets                                      4.1 非金融資產的減值
         At each reporting date or whenever a change in circumstances              於各報告日期或情況有變時，會考慮內部
         occurs, both internal and external sources of information are             及外來資料以評估資產有否出現減值跡
         considered to assess whether there is an indication that assets           象。倘出現有關跡象，可收回金額為資產
         are impaired. If such an indication exists, the recoverable               的公允價值減去處置成本和使用價值兩者
         amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of               中的較高者。倘資產之賬面值超出其可收
         disposal and value in use. If the carrying amount of an                   回金額，則會確認減值虧損以將資產扣減
         asset exceeds its recoverable amount, an impairment loss is               至其可收回金額。
         recognised to reduce the asset to its recoverable amount.

    4.2 Income tax                                                              4.2 所得稅

         calculations during the ordinary course of business for which             計算。倘最終稅務結果與初步記錄款額有
         the ultimate tax determination is uncertain. Where the final              別，差額將影響作出確定期間之即期及遞
         tax outcome of these matters is different from the amounts                延所得稅項撥備。
         that were initially recorded, such differences will impact the
         current and deferred income tax provisions in the period in
         which such determination is made.

         Deferred income tax assets relating to temporary differences              倘管理層考慮可能出現日後應課稅盈利而
         and tax losses are recognised when management considers                   須動用暫時差額或稅項虧損，即會確認與
         it is probable that future taxable profits will be available              暫時差額及稅項虧損相關之遞延所得稅項
         against the temporary differences or tax losses can be utilised.          資產。因本公司可控制及預先制定該等附
